- **mtu-blackhole-hunt**: Find a path MTU problem: "small requests work but large ones hang", "connection stalls after the TLS ClientHello", "only breaks over the VPN or in the overlay network", "ssh logs in but hangs on ls". The signature is a completed handshake and then large packets vanishing because ICMP fragmentation-needed is filtered, so path MTU discovery fails. Prove it with ping -M do -s N binary-searched to the largest payload that passes, tracepath, and a capture showing full-size segments retransmitted and never acknowledged. Compute tunnel overhead (WireGuard, IPsec, GRE, VXLAN, PPPoE) to match a cause.… ([greenblacked/AI](https://github.com/greenblacked/AI/tree/HEAD/plugins/linux/skills/mtu-blackhole-hunt))
- **mtu-mismatch-causing-silent-packet-dro-1e3259eff523a812**: Detects MTU blackholes where small packets pass but large ones vanish silently: probes with sized non-fragmenting pings to bisect the real path MTU, finds the offending hop, then fixes it with MSS clamping or a corrected interface MTU. Use when SSH hangs on large output, TLS handshakes stall, or VPNs pass only small packets. Not for total connectivity loss or DNS failures. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/mtu-mismatch-causing-silent-packet-dro-1e3259eff523a812))

- **mulmoterminal-decisions**: Check what this project's humans have already been asked, and how they answered, before asking them something similar.
